#2209. ​Arteta’s ‘dynasty’ dream collides with Arsenal’s harsh reality

Mikel Arteta says he wants to create a “dynasty” at Arsenal as he threw down the gauntlet to his players to retain their Premier League title and reach a “different level”.

Arsenal are on the verge of completing a £75m deal for Brazil midfielder Bruno Guimãraes from Newcastle and have not given up hope of landing Real Madrid winger Vinícius Júnior, who has been offered an improved contract by the Spanish giants.

The Premier League champions slumped to a 3-1 defeat to Real Betis in Dublin on Wednesday night, when they paid the price for individual errors and a lethargic, sloppy defensive display.

But that did little to quell Arteta’s ambitions for the new season. “I’m even more excited [about this season], and we’re going to go and attack every possible title because that’s the ambition that we have,” he said.

“We know that we have the ability. We will go from a strength to a strength as well, whether it’s with the players that we have to take them to a different level, and with the market that is still open to recruit as well, so we are very optimistic about it.

“All the work that we’ve done – that I’ve done through the summer – is to understand what we need to do to win again and to create a dynasty in this football club. That’s the aim.”

Despite Arteta’s bullishness, he conceded Arsenal would have to be a lot better than they were against Betis. When asked about the mood in the dressing room, he said: “Very upset, given a hard time. They are fuming, but this is good. That’s what we need.”

Telegraph Sport analyses some of the talking points from a disappointing evening at the Aviva Stadium.

The importance of goalkeeper David Raya and centre-backs William Saliba and Gabriel Magalhães to Arsenal hardly needs overstating. Their title success was built on the foundations provided by that triumvirate at the back.

Raya has yet to report back from holiday after Spain’s World Cup triumph and Saliba is facing an extended spell on the sidelines with a back injury. Gabriel did appear for the second half but, watching this, Arteta is unlikely to have been dissuaded from the belief he needs another top defender to compensate for the loss of Saliba.

It may have been only Arsenal’s second game of pre-season, so a degree of legginess was understandable.

But the ease with which they were cut open and overloaded by the Spanish side, and the volume of individual errors, did not sit comfortably with Arteta with just over a fortnight until they kick off their title defence at home to newly promoted Coventry City.

Only once in 63 matches last season did Arsenal concede three goals in a single game but they were fortunate Betis did not score more. “In the first half there are two different things: to play well and to compete,” said Arteta, who saw his side trail 3-1 at the interval.

“We did the first one quite well in a lot of moments but the compete part – especially in and around our box – was not to the standards to win a football match against a good side. It’s better to give them [the goals away] in a friendly match than an official match. It’s always good when you lose a match in pre-season because it creates a bit more fire in the belly.”

Kepa Arrizabalaga will not want to watch Betis’s first goal back in a hurry. The Arsenal goalkeeper may have got a slight nudge as he came for the ball at a corner but he was all over the place and never looked even in the vaguest control of the situation. Rodrigo Riquelme scored at the far post after the ball sailed over the keeper. Arteta called it a “really sloppy goal”. With former Leeds goalkeeper Illan Meslier having arrived on a free transfer as back-up to Raya, Kepa faces an uncertain future at Arsenal. Moments like this will not help his cause.

He did not feature much last season but is probably best remembered for his torrid showing in the Carabao Cup final defeat by Manchester City in March. He looked as unconvincing here as he did then, as the former Chelsea goalkeeper also sliced a clearance in the build up to Betis’s third goal. Before then, Kai Havertz had turned over possession cheaply from which Nelson Deossa set up former West Ham midfielder Pablo Fornals to score.

Arsenal need more creativity and firepower in attack, hence the interest in Vinícius and Atlético Madrid’s Julián Alvarez. Viktor Gyokeres again looked rather limited here and the likes of Bukayo Saka and Eberechi Eze are also still to report back. Yet one of the few, brighter aspects of Arsenal’s performances was the sight of the regal looking Riccardo Calafiori marauding forward from a very nominal left-back role.

If Arsenal are going to play more adventurously this season and try to ratchet up things in the entertainment stakes then Calafiori could have a big role to play, as long as Arteta can keep him fit. He can be a potent weapon galloping forward and there were clear glimpses of that here. Harnessing that talent while not compromising too much defensive security may be one of the manager’s challenges.

Louie Copley had only been on the pitch for nine minutes – a half-time substitute for Ben White – when the 19-year-old midfielder went down largely unchallenged with what looked like a troubling knee injury. Arteta admitted afterwards that the way the teenager fell to the ground was a concern and he looked in visible pain and distress as he was treated by the medical staff before being helped off the pitch.

The fear will be of some kind of ligament damage and Arteta confirmed Copley would be scanned on Thursday. “Quite worried to be fair for the kid because the mechanism doesn’t look very positive and the symptoms that he already has,” the manager said. “So we are going to have to wait and see for tomorrow’s scan. Touch wood that is not a bad one.”

“It is another opportunity for fitness. Coming into games against teams of good quality, it is important we get good fitness. It is a chance to build momentum for the season ahead. We are taking steps in the right direction. Of course, we want more from the performance and the results, but we take learnings and move on.

“All of us, together, we share a common goal. We have confidence to build on. We will take every game as it comes. The atmosphere was amazing. I did not realise Ireland has so many Arsenal fans. We are so grateful for everyone who came to support us today. We wanted to give them a good result and performance. Hopefully next time we can.”

“We always want to win and in the end we could not today. In some ways they deserved it and we could have done better. We did have some good moments though. We had an amazing season last season. I believe in this team and there are still players to come back from the World Cup. I am confident but we can always do better.”

There is the final whistle in Dublin and Betis secure a 3-1 win over Arsenal at Aviva Stadium. Goals from Riquelme, Deossa and Fornals has led Manuel Pellegrini’s side to the victory. All four goals in the game came before the break.

Nwaneri catches Bernal and if this was a competitive fixture than I think Nwaneri would perhaps have been booked for that challenge.

Arsenal have a corner early on in this second half as Vieira’s shot from distance deflects behind. The delivery from the left has too much on it, going over the heads of all the Arsenal players in the box, and Betis are able to clear.

Mikel Arteta has made a whole host of changes, which includes the likes of Jesus, Martinelli, Gabriel and Vieira coming on. Betis have brought the former Real Madrid man Isco on at the break.

Arsenal’s title triumph was built on that stout defence and conceding so few but there have been so many self-inflicted wounds here. Arsenal are closing in on the signing of Newcastle and Brazil midfielder Bruno Guimaraes in a deal worth £75m. He has been given permission to undergo a medical today and is set to join Arsenal in the coming days. One midfielder coming into Arsenal is set to see another depart as Dane Christian Norgaard closes in on a move to Everton after just one season at Arsenal.

August 1- Girona 1 Arsenal 4Today- Arsenal vs Real BetisAugust 9- Arsenal vs Borussia DortmundAugust 12- Arsenal vs ComoAugust 16- Arsenal vs Manchester City (Community Shield)

Our starting XI for #ArsenalRealBetis! 💚🤍 pic.twitter.com/8rRvy9IZCm

🟡 𝗧𝗘𝗔𝗠𝙉𝙀𝙒𝙎 🔵 Unchanged from last time out.Let's keep our good pre-season form going ✊🤝 Presented by @deel pic.twitter.com/S5TKqR9Lv3

Premier League champions Arsenal continue their preparations for the new season as they take on Spanish side Real Betis at the Aviva Stadium in Dublin, with this being the first-ever meeting between the two sides. This is the first time Arsenal have played a pre-season game in Dublin since August 2018, when they drew 1-1 with London rivals Chelsea, with Arsenal winning on penalties.

Arsenal beat Girona 4-1 in their opening game of pre-season, with new signing Christos Tzolis on the scoresheet in his first appearance for the club. After today, Arsenal have three more games, including the Community Shield, before they kick off the defence of their Premier League crown at home against newly-promoted Coventry on opening night of the new season. They are closing in on a deal for Newcastle captain Bruno Guimaraes, who is set to become one of Arsenal’s most expensive signings. Mikel Arteta’s side have been dealt a blow ahead of the season with the news that France centre-back William Saliba will miss an extended period due to a back injury. The likes of Declan Rice, Bukayo Saka and Noni Madueke are still yet to return after their World Cup exploits.

Real Betis, under the management of former Manchester City boss Manuel Pellegrini, finished fifth in La Liga last season and have therefore qualified for this coming season’s Champions League after a 20-year absence from the competition. Pellegrini has led Betis into European competitions for five consecutive seasons, which includes losing the 2025 Europa Conference League final to Chelsea. They have been busy in pre-season action already having played five games up to this point. They have won four of the five, including a 4-0 victory over Lyon, with just one defeat coming against Granada.

They have a number of former Premier League players in their ranks, including Antony, Giovani Lo Celso, Pablo Fornals and former Arsenal man Hector Bellerin. Real Betis have already played a number of friendlies as they build up to the start of their season, which begins on August 21 when they take on Real Sociedad.

#2208. Il 12 novembre al Maschio Angioino l’evento «Mezzogiorno, Napoli per l’alternativa» con l’intervento di Nicola Fratoianni

12 NOVEMBRE A NAPOLI L’EVENTO: MEZZOGIORNO, NAPOLI PER L’ALTERNATIVA Sinistra Italiana a Napoli: “Mezzogiorno, Napoli per l’alternativa”. Il 12 novembre al Maschio Angioino il dibattito sul futuro del Sud con Nicola Fratoianni NAPOLI – In un momento storico decisivo per il futuro del Mezzogiorno e del Paese, Sinistra Italiana promuove un appuntamento politico di primario rilievo: l’incontro pubblico dal titolo “Mezzogiorno, Napoli per l’alternativa”, che si terrà giovedì 12 novembre alle ore 17:30 nella prestigiosa cornice della Sala della Loggia presso il Maschio Angioino. L’incontro ha il fine di tracciare una strada chiara per il riscatto sociale ed economico del Sud, mettendo al centro le battaglie storiche per il lavoro, la giustizia sociale, i diritti ed il contrasto alle diseguaglianze territoriali. L’incontro vedrà la partecipazione delle principali voci delle istituzioni, dell’avvocatura e della politica locale e nazionale: Introduce: Gianluca Daniele (già Consigliere Regionale). Presiede: Pietro Rinaldi (R.U. Armena Sviluppo e Responsabile Enti Locali AVS). Modera: Ilaria Urbani (Giornalista de La Repubblica). Interventi programmati: Stefano Loffredo – Segretario Provinciale della Federazione di Napoli di Sinistra Italiana Toti Lange – Consigliere Comunale Annalisa Senese – Avvocata penalista Daniele Cutolo – Presidente Merqurio e avvocato Peppe De Cristofaro – Capogruppo AVS al Senato. Le conclusioni saranno affidate a Nicola Fratoianni, Segretario Nazionale di Sinistra Italiana e Deputato per l’Alleanza Verdi e Sinistra.

#2207. Betis, Riquelme finisce nel mirino di due inglesi. Il club chiede almeno 20 milioni

A gennaio potrebbe andare in scena una sfida tutta inglese per l’esterno offensivo Rodrigo Riquelme del Real Betis di Siviglia. Il classe 2000 è infatti uno dei protagonisti dell’avvio di stagione dei biancoverdi con cui ha già messo a segno tre reti in 11 presenze ne LaLiga attirando così l’interesse di alcuni club anglosassoni.

In particolare, come riporta 4-4-2.com, sarebbero Fulham e Covntry City a aver messo nel mirino Riquelme in vista del mercato di riparazione pur non avendo ancora presentato un’offerta formale al Betis. L’attaccante ha un contratto fino al 2030 con il club andaluso che lo ha pagato 7,5 milioni dall’Aletico Madrid e che ora potrebbe mettere a segno un’importante plusvalenza. Sembra infatti che il Betis abbia intenzione di chiedere non meno di 20 milioni di euro per avviare l’eventuale trattativa per la cessione del calciatore che in passato ha già militato in Inghilterra vestendo per 19 volte la maglia del Bournemouth con cui ha segnato anche due reti in Championship nel 2020/21.

#2206. Scuole evacuate a Roma dopo nuovi allarmi bomba via email

Tra gli istituti coinvolti figurano il Giovanni Cagliero di Largo Volumnia, il Petrarca di via Ceneda e il Rodari di via Norcia.

Allarme bomba nelle scuole, evacuazioni a Roma: coinvolti numerosi istituti

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Un’unica mail ha fatto scattare un allarme bomba in oltre 100 scuole italiane, provocando evacuazioni e operazioni di bonifica in decine di istituti di ogni ordine e grado. La situazione riguarda anche Roma, dove l’allerta è scattata in numerose strutture.

Nella Capitale sono state interessate diverse scuole del quartiere Appio-Tuscolano. Gli studenti sono stati fatti uscire dagli edifici e sarebbero in corso le operazioni necessarie per trasferirli in luoghi sicuri.

La mail che ha dato origine agli allarmi risulta firmata “Mikhail Andropov”. Al momento sono in corso le evacuazioni.

Secondo quanto riportato da Adnkronos, tra gli istituti romani coinvolti figurano diverse scuole della zona Appio-Tuscolano.

Tra le strutture interessate c’è l’Istituto Comprensivo Giovanni Cagliero, in Largo Volumnia.

L’allarme riguarda anche l’Istituto Petrarca di via Ceneda e l’Istituto Rodari di via Norcia.

Tra gli istituti coinvolti viene indicata inoltre la scuola secondaria di primo grado Sibari, situata nei pressi di Porta Metronia.

L’evacuazione non ha riguardato soltanto questi istituti. Sono state infatti fatte evacuare anche alcune scuole primarie e asili nido della zona.

L’allarme non riguarda esclusivamente Roma. La stessa mail avrebbe infatti interessato oltre 100 scuole in tutta Italia, facendo scattare controlli, bonifiche ed evacuazioni in decine di istituti di ogni ordine e grado.

Nelle scuole romane interessate dall’allerta, gli studenti sono stati allontanati dalle strutture.

Al momento sarebbero in corso le operazioni per il loro trasferimento in luoghi sicuri, mentre proseguono le procedure legate all’allarme.

Una situazione che coinvolge dunque numerosi istituti italiani e che, nella Capitale, interessa in particolare diverse scuole dell’area Appio-Tuscolano.

#2204. Inter, si chiude un'era dopo 29 anni: addio a Inter Campus. Moratti e Tronchetti vanno avanti da soli

Inter, si chiude un'era dopo 29 anni: addio a Inter Campus. Moratti e Tronchetti vanno avanti da soli

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Inter Campus non fa più parte dell'Inter. La notizia arriva dal quotidiano La Stampa che racconta come dall'estate scorsa il progetto fondato da Massimo Moratti nel 1997 non faccia più parte del percorso del club. La fondazione benefica è parte della storia nerazzurra degli ultimi 30 anni ma è un progetto che si distacca ormai da qualche mese dall'Inter.

Scegli Fcinter1908.it come tuo sito preferito su Google: clicca qui

"Dal 21 luglio scorso il progetto Inter Campus è portato avanti esclusivamente dalla Fondazione We are the Campus, creata dalle famiglie Moratti e Tronchetti Provera. Nel board siedono Massimo e Milly Moratti con i figli Carlotta, Celeste, Maria, Angelomario e Giovanni; Marco Troncetti Provera con il figlio Giovanni", si legge nell'articolo pubblicato dal quotidiano.

"La decisione è nata dopo che l’Inter, in seguito all’avvento del Fondo Oaktree, ha scelto di modificare il suo approccio alle attività benefiche creando Fondazione Inter che ha preso il posto di Inter Futura, il veicolo societario guidato da Carlotta Moratti nel quale rientrava Inter Campus".

"La nuova Fondazione Inter si propone di svolgere attività benefiche in Italia e nel mondo “tramite erogazioni di risorse economiche, beni o servizi”. Inter Campus, invece, vive grazie a una presenza tangibile in 31 Paesi del mondo con 300 operatori locali al servizio di bambini bisognosi dai 6 ai 13 anni", spiega ancora il quotidiano.

Inter Campus è un progetto premiato più volte dall'ONU per la funzione sociale che svolge attraverso il calcio ed è uno dei progetti di solidarietà e beneficenza più noti nel mondo. Opera in diversi Paesi: Africa, Sud America, Europa dell'Est, Asia.

"I Bambini di 31 paesi in tutto il Mondo ringraziano l'Inter che li ha accompagnati nella magnifica avventura di Inter Campus per 30 anni. D'ora in poi continueranno ad esprimere la loro felicità con la nuova Fondazione We Are The Campus. Ci dispiace non poter più giocare in nome dell'Inter, che comunque rimarrà nel nostro cuore, così come sulle nostre maglie”, si legge in un comunicato diffuso in queste ore da Massimo Moratti e da sua figlia Carlotta e che viene riportato nell'articolo del giornale torinese.

"Di fatto questa separazione chiude anche l'ultimo legame societario rimasto tra Moratti e l'Inter. La notizia colpisce ancora di più perché pochi giorni fa nel report di sostenibilità pubblicato dall’Inter, a corredo del bilancio, Inter Campus veniva elogiato come uno dei pilastri dell'impegno civile del club nerazzurro", spiega il quotidiano. L'ultimo bilancio pubblicato risale alla scorsa stagione e We are The Campus è nato nel 2026. Resterà comunque il nome Inter sulle magliette nerazzurre dei bambini di We are The Campus che sarà gestito dalla famiglia di Massimo Moratti e da quella di Tronchetti-Provera.

#2203. Fabiani svela la scelta di Gattuso: “Prima opzione, non abbiamo avuto dubbi”

Il ds racconta la scelta di Gattuso: prima opzione di Lazio e Lotito. Poi l’elogio al tecnico della capolista.

Lazio, Fabiani esalta Gattuso: “Sta dirigendo alla grande l’orchestra”

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Gennaro Gattuso era la prima scelta della Lazio. Nessun ripiego e, soprattutto, nessun dubbio da parte del direttore sportivo Angelo Fabiani e del presidente Claudio Lotito. A raccontare i retroscena della scelta dell’allenatore è stato lo stesso dirigente biancoceleste.

Fabiani, parlando della Lazio capolista di Serie A, ha riservato parole importanti a Gattuso. Il ds ne ha sottolineato soprattutto la capacità di rapportarsi con calciatori e dirigenti, utilizzando poi una metafora significativa: “Gli abbiamo dato la bacchetta e lui sta dirigendo alla grande l’orchestra”.

Secondo quanto riportato da La Gazzetta dello Sport, Fabiani ha spiegato innanzitutto quanto sia importante scegliere un tecnico nel momento più adatto: “Gli allenatori sono tutti giusti, ma vanno anche presi al momento giusto”.

L’esperienza maturata nei diversi club della sua carriera ha portato Fabiani a una convinzione precisa sul ruolo dell’allenatore.

“Ne ho avuti tanti nei vari club in cui ho lavorato. Chi non è andato bene è solo perché era arrivato nel momento sbagliato”.

Una situazione che, secondo il direttore sportivo, non riguarda certamente Gattuso. La Lazio aveva infatti individuato subito in Rino il profilo sul quale puntare.

“È stata la nostra prima opzione. Lotito è un presidente che lascia ampia libertà sul mercato, sulla scelta dell’allenatore però è presente”.

Fabiani ha quindi chiarito come la decisione sia stata condivisa con il presidente biancoceleste: “Ma io e lui non abbiamo avuto dubbi nel puntare su Rino. Le sue grandi qualità le conoscevamo bene”.

Il dirigente della Lazio è entrato poi nel dettaglio delle caratteristiche che maggiormente apprezza dell’allenatore.

“Gattuso è stato un campione formidabile, che ha vinto tutto”.

Il valore aggiunto, però, secondo Fabiani va ricercato soprattutto nel modo in cui il tecnico riesce a gestire i rapporti all’interno del club.

“Ma la sua grandezza è mettere tutto questo da parte nel rapporto con i giocatori e con i dirigenti”.

Da qui la frase con cui il direttore sportivo sintetizza il lavoro svolto finora dal tecnico alla guida della Lazio:

“Noi gli abbiamo dato la bacchetta e lui sta dirigendo alla grande l’orchestra”.

#2200. Manna a KKN: “Tanti infortuni, ma non devono essere un alibi. Pensiamo di aggregare Neres, Favasuli e Spinazzola da sabato”

Giovanni Manna, direttore sportivo del Napoli, ha parlato a Radio Kiss Kiss Napoli: “Emergenza infortuni? Ci attendono molte gare alla ripresa. Il periodo non è dei più semplici, non abbiamo mai avuto la rosa a disposizione ma questo non deve essere un alibi, anche le altre squadre hanno subìto infortuni. Dobbiamo crescere e lavorare partita dopo partita. Sicuramente ci sono stati degli aspetti sia positivi che negativi. Dopo la vittoria di Monza e la grande prestazione contro la Roma, le sconfitte di Oporto e Torino ci hanno tolto un po’ di certezze. Contro la Lazio è stata una sfida complicata, i biancocelesti hanno giocato più liberi mentalmente, la reazione che abbiamo avuto nel secondo tempo è fortemente incoraggiante. C’è un Napoli ha voglia di uscire da questo momento. Speriamo di dare una linfa nuova alla squadra con la ripresa. Parte della squadra è libera fino a sabato. Abbiamo preferito non spezzettare il periodo di riposo, è stato un periodo molto intenso. Stiamo recuperando dei calciatori. Pensiamo di aggregare Neres, Favasuli e Spinazzola da sabato. Speriamo di accelerare e recuperare gradualmente Badiashile, Di Lorenzo e Alisson sperando di non incorrere in situazioni spiacevoli. Lavoriamo e proviamo a recuperare le energie. Siamo assolutamente consci della situazione, non siamo contenti di questo avvio di stagione. Se abbiamo sbagliato e fatto delle valutazioni non corrette, vogliamo lavorare e risolvere. Abbiamo sempre cercato di onorare la maglia, sono sicuro che usciremo insieme e uniti da questa situazione. Le critiche sono state troppo strumentalizzate. Vogliamo lavorare ed essere uniti, speriamo di fare un filotto di vittorie per i nostri tifosi. Sicuramente le ultime sfide hanno presentato prestazioni di livello. Nella vita, come nel calcio, i dettagli e l’attenzione fanno la differenza. Se non siamo riusciti a recuperare e portare a casa il risultato finale, qualcosa abbiamo sbagliato. Dobbiamo capire dove crescere e ritrovare continuità. Dobbiamo fare meglio e lavorare meglio. Mercato? Con il mister e il presidente ci confrontiamo ogni giorno. In questo momento, qualche precauzione la troviamo sempre. Abbiamo le idee chiare, cercheremo di migliorare la rosa. Dobbiamo però arrivarci a gennaio: ci sono tante partite. Dobbiamo dare continuità di risultati. Lo scorso anno abbiamo speso tanto, forse dovevamo spendere meglio, lo dico anche facendo autocritica. Col presidente non ci siamo mai tirati indietro. Abbiamo dovuto rispettare dei parametri. Nessuno ci ha vietato di vendere o comprare, semplicemente non ci siamo riusciti. A gennaio ci faremo trovare pronti. Le idee chiare le avevamo anche prima, nell’anno post scudetto abbiamo anche fatto 12-13 acquisti. Poi, è chiaro, c’è sempre qualcuno che esplode e chi meno. Cacciamani? Spesso si spendono cifre folli per chi non ha ancora dimostrato nulla. Dobbiamo stare attenti. Stiamo seguendo diversi giocatori. Il panorama italiano è ricco, ci sono giocatori interessanti, vogliamo dare loro opportunità. Guardiamo a tutti i campionati. Spesso escono diversi nomi, alcuni fuorvianti. Che giochi in Spagna, Francia o Inghilterra, l’innesto che ci servirà deve dare una mano. Ripeto, però: dobbiamo prima arrivare a gennaio! Varela? E’ un buon giocatore, ma attualmente abbiamo Hojlund, Lucca e Giovane che può essere impiegato nel ruolo di centravanti. Il portoghese sta performando, è difficile che il Monza si privi di un giocatore così. Se lo dovesse fare, non lo regalerà di certo. Il Napoli, in questo momento, non ha fatto alcun passo con nessuno”.

I Am Naples Testata Giornalistica - aut. #2199. Gomes scores for Aston Villa in defeat by Bayern Munich in Hong Kong

Aston Villa’s tour of the Far East ended in defeat as they lost 2-1 to German champions Bayern Munich in a pre-season friendly at the Kai Tak Sports Park in Hong Kong on Friday.

Bayern took the lead inside the final 10 minutes of the first half, with defender Kim Min-jae heading in from Tom Bischof’s sublime free-kick. Villa goalkeeper Marco Bizot made a string of saves in the second half to keep Bayern’s lead at one goal until former Liverpool forward Luis Díaz smashed in Bayern’s second off the underside of the bar.

Just as the game appeared to be out of reach for Aston Villa, who had struggled to create much going forward for most of the game, Unai Emery’s side showed some life late on. Summer signing João Gomes’s goal from the edge of the box gave them hope before Tammy Abraham nearly brought them back onto level terms, only to be denied by a fine save from 40-year-old Manuel Neuer.

Aston Villa are next in action in six days’ time when they take on Paris St-Germain in the Uefa Super Cup before kicking off their Premier League campaign at Brighton. Bayern have one more friendly to come against RB Leipzig before the DFL-Supercup against Borussia Dortmund. They then kick off the defence of their Bundesliga crown against Stuttgart at home on August 28.

Stepping up in the big moments! 🐐🧤 pic.twitter.com/r14rF39XpR

Unai Emery’s men head home from the Far East to face PSG in the Uefa Super Cup in Salzburg next week and then have one more pre-season friendly against Borussia Monchengladbach before starting their Premier League campaign at Brighton.

Possession: Aston Villa 44%-56% Bayern MunichShots: 8-16Shots on target: 3-8Corners: 3-7

Bizot nearly has a calamitous moment coming out of his penalty area but gets away with it.

The final whistle is blown soon after and Bayern hold on to beat Villa 2-1 in Hong Kong. They had looked comfortable when Diaz put them 2-0 up but Villa kept going until the end.

Victory in the final match of the #AudiFCBTour! 💪🔥 pic.twitter.com/UFhQhme62i

The space opens up for Palhinha to shoot from range but Bizot makes a fairly comfortable save down to his right.

It was all Bayern for 80 minutes of this game but Villa have come to life in the closing stages, with Vincent Kompany’s side now holding on a bit.

When Diaz scored Bayern’s second goal, Aston Villa were out of this game but it has been all Villa since then. They look the more likely to get a goal.

Gomes’ free-kick out on the left is parried behind by Neuer for a Villa corner on the right. The corner comes to nothing.

For 80 minutes, Aston Villa barely looked like scoring but come so close to getting two goals in just a few minutes. Abraham meets a cross from the right and must think he has scored but his header is brilliantly saved by the 40-year-old Neuer.

Maybe this game is not quite done and dusted yet. A cross from the right picks out Gomes on the edge of the box. The Brazilian takes one touch before toe poking past Neuer and into the Bayern net. Gomes meant that finish and Villa have some hope.

Abraham should have done better there. He has the space to drive down Villa’s right and has a decision to make; cross the ball to his left and drive forward himself to shoot. He takes a while to make the decision before his attempted cross is intercepted by Ito.

Boey’s yellow card was his final act of the game as he is replaced by Della Rovere. Boey did seem to be struggling with some cramp.

Bayern receive their second yellow card of the game as Boey is booked for cynically pulling down Lynch.

Cash, Maatsen and Buendia have gone off for Aston Villa.

Double substitution from Bayern as Pavic and Assomo replace Tah and Brown.

A fantastic goal to put the Germans 2-0 up and it is nothing more than they deserve. Ibrahimovic finds Diaz in space on the left and the Colombian drives at Cash. As he enters the Villa box, Diaz cuts inside onto his preferred right foot and smashes a shot towards goal. It appeared to go right over Bizot, who opts not to put a hand up. The ball crashes off the underside of the bar and goes in. It has been all Bayern in this second half and that goal had been coming.

Bayern are knocking on the door and come very close to doubling their lead. Diaz’s shot from the edge of the box looks destined to hit the net with Bizot beaten but fortunately for Villa Abraham is on hand on his own goal line to make the block.

Bizot makes another great save. Bayern win a free-kick out on the right after Brown was fouled. Bischoff opts to shoot instead of crossing and nearly beats Bizot at his near post but the Aston Villa keeper gets across to his left to make the save.

Unai Emery makes a double substitution as Mings and Lynch replace Torres and Guessand. Mings takes the captain’s armband.

That is a fantastic double save from Bizot, first denying Stanisic and then Ibrahimovic. Both of those shots came through bodies so Bizot did a good job of reacting and making the saves with his legs on both occasions.

It is safe to say the Bayern fans out in Hong Kong love Diaz, who is cheered every time he touches the ball.

Vincent Kompany is going to make a triple change as Ito, Diaz and Palhinha come on. Former Liverpool man Diaz received a great ovation from the fans in Hong Kong. Palhinha, who spent last season on loan at Tottenha, has been linked with a move to Aston Villa in this window.

Should Bayern have been awarded a penalty there? A ball is played in behind the Villa defence into the penalty area and Ibrahimovic appeared to be brought down but the referee waves those appeals away. On first viewing, that appeared to be a penalty.

Just before the hour mark, it is time for some changes from Aston Villa. Abraham and Bogarde come on for Madjo and Kamara. This is Abraham’s first appearance of this pre-season.

Good play from Gomes earns Aston Villa a free-kick not far outside the Bayern penalty area out on the left, in a similar position to the free-kick that Bayern scored from in the first half. Buendia clips in a good ball to the back post, where Guessand looks likely to score but instead misses the ball on the stretch. Should Guessand have done better with that? Probably.

Brown was starting in his customary left-back position for Bayern in the first half but is now operating out on the right wing.

Gomes leaves one late on Pavlovic and, had this been a competitive fixture, I suspect that might have been a yellow card.

Tah is brought down by Gomes and Bayern will have a free-kick out on the right not a million miles outside the Villa penalty area. The delivery though is poor and easily cleared away.

Bayern have brought Stanisic on at half-time for Laimer. Aston Villa have not made a change at the break.

Possession: Aston Villa 45%-55% Bayern MunichShots: 4-7Shots on target: 1-2Corners: 1-2

The first half in Hong Kong is brought to an end and Bayern take a 1-0 lead into the break courtesy of Kim’s goal.

There will be one added minute at the end of this first half.

Aston Villa have struggled to create much going forward and have not really had a genuine goal-scoring opportunity in this first half. Their forward line though will look quite different when they kick off the seaosn than it does today.

The German side take the lead in Hong Kong. Bischof’s left-footed delivery from a free-kick out on the left is sublime, whipping it right into the corridor of uncertainty. The quality of delivery means it is quite a simple header for Kim, who makes no mistake with his header into the far corner. Kim might have been offside but obviously there is no VAR in use today. 1-0 Bayern.

That is a waste in the end from Bayern. Boey fires a pass in-field into Laimer, who takes a fantastic first touch on the turn inside the Villa half. He drives through the Villa half and at the penalty area but his pass outside looking for Cardozo is poor and overhit, with the ball rolling out for a goal kick. Laimer will be annoyed at himself having done all the hard work and then failed to execute the easy part.

Bischof eyes one up from range and tries to pick out the top corner. Bizot is routed to his spot and is hoping the shot goes wide. Bischof’s effort is always curling wide, much to Bizot’s relief.

The first booking of the game goes the way of Bayern Munich. Guessand drives forward into the Bayern half and is brought down by a cynical foul from Cardozo. You were half expecting Cardozo to perhaps escape without a booking with it being a pre-season friendly but he does not. It was the right call to book him.

That was lovely from Pavlovic. The German midfielder shows good feet to beat Gomes before getting his shot away from a central position just outside the box. He forces Bizot into a decent save but one he should be making.

Bayern force Villa into an error and win themselves a corner as a result out on the left. It is delivered into a dangerous area at the front post but Villa do a good job of handling it and clearing their lines.

Bayern Munich have made two signings this summer; Brown from Bundesliga rivals Frankfurt and Saibari from PSV. Brown is starting at left-back for Bayern today with Saibari not involved as he closes in on a return from a knock picked up at the World Cup with Morocco. Brown feels like such a Bayern signing, nabbing a good player from a Bundesliga rival.

Aston Villa have made four signings so far this summer but just one is in their starting XI today in the form of Gomes, who joined from Midlands rivals Wolves this summer. Young Swiss midfielder Manzambi has joined from Freiburg but is currently out injured. Garnacho picked up a knock and Cisse, who has arrived from LASK, is not involved for Villa today.

The ball is played in behind the Aston Villa defence to pick out the run of Binder. On the edge of the Villa box, Binder gets the first touch but Lindelof is calm in defence and heads back to his keeper Bizot.

Moments later, Gomes is dispossessed inside his own half and Ibrahimovic has a shot from just outside the box but drags it wide of the far post.

Good work from 17-year-old Madjo down Aston Villa’s left wins them their first corner of the game. Buendia’s inswinger is headed away too easily at the near post. Aston Villa have seen their former set-piece coach Austin MacPhee nabbed this summer by Xabi Alonso’s Chelsea.

The referee is allowing the game to flow and thus not blowing for soft fouls.

The first chance of the game falls to Bayern Munich. Brown’s cross from the left is headed by Maatsen straight to Ibrahimovic on the edge of the box. Ibrahimovic, not any relation to Zlatan, takes the shot on first time but it is blocked by Maatsen, who makes up for that poor header with the block. The ball goes behind for a corner, which comes to nothing.

Interestingly, there are only eight outfield substitutions on the Aston Villa bench for this game, with the likes of Garnacho and McGinn picking up knocks the other day. It appears then that at least a couple of Villa outfield players will have to play the full 90.

Both sides emerge from the tunnel, Aston Villa led out by Matty Cash and Bayern Munich by Manuel Neuer, and we are closing in on kick-off in Hong Kong. A group of delegates shake the hands of both starting XIs.

Chelsea and Juventus played in this stadium the other night and it is safe to say that the pitch does not look in the best state ahead of kick-off today. It looks like it will continue to rip up and the last thing the two managers will want is to pick up any serious injuries.

Currently, before the two sides emerge, the crowd in Hong Kong are being treated to some pre-match dancing and entertainment, which includes people dressed as pandas and sharks dancing. Would the Premier League ever take inspiration and have dancing pandas before kick-off?

Aston Villa: Bizot, Cash, Lindelof, Torres, Maatsen, Kamara, Joao Gomes, Hemmings, Buendia, Guessand, Madjo.Substitutes: Wright, Asemota, Mings, Abraham, Nedejkovic, Bogarde, Rowe, Burrowes, Carroll, Lynch.

Bayern Munich: Neuer, Kim, Tah, Brown, Boey, Laimer, Bischof, Pavlovic, Ibrahimovic, Binder, Cardozo. Substitutes: Ulrich, Podar-Stiube, Urbig, Kimmich, Luis Díaz, Palhinha, Ito, Chávez, Pavic, Bara, Manuba, Stanisic, Della Rovere, Assomo.

The likes of Ollie Watkins, Ezri Konsa and Emi Martinez are yet to return to training after their World Cup exploits and Aston Villa are also without captain John McGinn and new signing Alejandro Garnacho, who picked up knocks in their win over BG Pathum the other day.

Aston Villa have already sold Youri Tielemans and Morgan Rogers this summer, with Lucas Digne on the verge of a move to PSG, but could another starter be on the way out of Villa Park? England defender Ezri Konsa has been linked with moves to the likes of Arsenal and Liverpool but will Unai Emery be able to keep hold of the 28-year-old?

The atmosphere is building with our fans in Hong Kong! 🔴⚪️#AudiFCBTour pic.twitter.com/m4fFXAWxGb

INS:Johan Manzambi ⇠ Freiburg, £50mJoao Gomes ⇠ Wolves, £34mAlejandro Garnacho ⇠ Chelsea, LoanModou Keba Cisse ⇠ LASK, £5m

OUTS:Youri Tielemans ⇢ Manchester United, £36mDonyell Malen ⇢ Roma, £20mMorgan Rogers ⇢ Chelsea, £117mLewis Dobbin ⇢ Southampton, £10mEnzo Barrenechea ⇢ Benfica, £10

It has been a big summer of change at Aston Villa already and there could be more changes before the end of the window. Left-back Lucas Digne is set to move to PSG and is set to be replaced by Atlético Madrid’s Matteo Ruggeri. Joao Palhinha, who is on the bench for Bayern Munich today, has been linked with a move to Aston Villa.

“Aston Villa are a top side with a very good coach. They have a large squad and will field plenty of talent. They have already played more friendlies than us, but for us it is a good challenge. It is a match in which we will learn a lot about our team’s fitness. It will be a good match for us.”

“It is difficult to improve; after all, it is not just about the trophies we have won, but also about the number of goals and matches we have won. It is possible, but difficult. Right now, I would say our aim is to win the Champions League; there is no doubt about that. And throughout the season, I will be saying that we need to focus on the next match. To achieve that; to win the Bundesliga, the cup and the Champions League, we need the whole squad to be fit and healthy. We need to make good use of them and improve the squad. We have already done that, but hopefully it will help us achieve our goals.”

Your Aston Villa team to face Bayern Munich in Hong Kong 👊 pic.twitter.com/VxyopTRMFp

July 21- Walsall 0 Aston Villa 5July 25- Porto 2 Aston Villa 1July 28- Aston Villa 2 Real Sociedad 4August 1- Indonesia All Stars 1 Aston Villa 3August 4- BG Pathum 1 Aston Villa 3Today- Aston Villa vs Bayern MunichAugust 12- PSG vs Aston Villa (Uefa Super Cup)August 15- Borussia Monchengladbach vs Aston Villa

Aston Villa’s preparations for the new season continue out in Hong Kong as they take on Bundesliga champions Bayern Munich at the Kai Tak Stadium. These sides have met twice before; Aston Villa beat Bayern 1-0 to win the European Cup in 1981/82 before then meeting in the Champions League group stages during the 2024/25 season, with Jhon Duran’s long-range screamer giving Aston Villa a 1-0 win at Villa Park.

After losing two of their first three pre-season friendlies, Aston Villa have won their first two games on Asian soil and this game marks the end of their tour of the Far East. It has been a summer of significant change at Aston Villa thus far with some key members of their squad departing and some new faces arriving. Youri Tielemans has departed after Manchester United triggered his release clause and they then sold Morgan Rogers to Chelsea for a British transfer record £117m. Unai Emery’s side have brought in 20-year-old Swiss midfielder Johan Manzambi, Brazilian midfielder Joao Gomes and Alejandro Garnacho, with the deal for Garnacho being an initial loan. Manzambi is currently on the sidelines due to a knee injury.

Unai Emery’s side were dealt a blow during the World Cup as Belgian midfielder Amadou Onana picked up a serious knee injury. They were though boosted by the return of Boubacar Kamara after seven months out as he scored in the 3-1 friendly victory over BG Parthum. As one midfielder returned from injury in that game, another picked up a fresh injury as John McGinn suffered a knock to his knee. The likes of Ollie Watkins, Ezri Konsa and Emi Martinez are yet to return after their world Cup exploits. They begin their Premier League campaign with an away game on the south coast against Brighton on August 23 but will be involved in the Uefa Super Cup against PSG next week having lifted the Europa League last season.

Bayern Munich head into this season looking to win a third straight Bundesliga title and have won two of their three friendlies, losing once. They have added Moroccan attacker Ismael Saibari from PSV and German left-back Nathaniel Brown from Bundesliga rivals Eintracht Frankfurt to their ranks this summer. The likes of Harry Kane, Dayot Upamecano and Michael Olise are not available as they are still yet to return after World Cup duty but Vincent Kompany has confirmed that Bayern’s German stars are set to return.

After today, Bayern have one more friendly against RB Leipzig before the DFL-Supercup against Borussia Dortmund. They then kick off the defence of their Bundesliga crown against Stuttgart at home on August 28.
